  16: - agent-docs/DECISIONS.md
  17: SYNTH_CLAUDE_FINAL_B

===== agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION_RPR-0.6.md =====
   1: # Replay / Production Business-Logic Parity
   2: 
   3: **Revision:** RPR-0.6-DRAFT  
   4: **Status:** NOT APPROVED ƒ?" IMPLEMENTATION FORBIDDEN  
   5: **Baseline:** `0cb6a9a6cc64ba8ac9d33797bcc30af534fd46e3`; the complete dirty-worktree snapshot must be re-hashed and approved immediately before implementation.  
   6: **Supersedes:** RPR-0.4/RPR-0.5 for review only.
   7: 
   8: ## 1. Business objective and flow
   9: 
  10: Replay must use current Production paper-trading policies with an isolated
  11: virtual account. It must never read Production account state, call HTTP, or
  12: call candle-only output historical parity.
  13: 
  14: 1. User selects one symbol, virtual profile, window and mode.
  15: 2. Qualifier validates every input before a generated Replay artifact exists.
  16: 3. A refusal writes only a run/certificate diagnostic.
  17: 4. Qualified analysis uses closed, identity-matching candles.
  18: 5. Simulation invokes pure Production policies with only virtual adapters.
  19: 6. Results stage privately and publish only on terminal success.
  20: 
  21: Release 1 labels outcomes only `ANALYSIS_PARITY`,
  22: `PRODUCTION_LOGIC_SIMULATION`, or `REFUSED_INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE`.
  23: `ENTRY_DECISION_PARITY` and historical wallet-lifecycle parity are out of scope:
  24: they require account-state evidence that Replay is forbidden to read.
  25: 
  26: ## 2. Root cause, evidence and scope
  27: 
  28: Replay currently can include open candles, has null IDs, reads
  29: `wallet_settings`, and differs from Production price/signal management.
  30: Evidence is current source in `RegressionTestWorker`,
  31: `ShadowProductionReplayService`, `ExecutionReplayScope`,
  32: `TradeExecutionValidationService`, `DynamicProfitLockService`,
  33: `LivePositionProtectionService`, `PositionManagementService`, and
  34: `PaperTradingService`.
  35: 
  36: Close-message lineage, deployed JAR/config identity and historical
  37: order-book/derivative/sentiment availability are not durable for all old
  38: windows. Strict claims are therefore unreachable for such eras unless a future
  39: approved forward-capture design is implemented.
  40: 
  41: In scope: single-symbol virtual simulation; qualifier/refusal; closed-candle
  42: identity; virtual settings/constraints; stable Replay IDs; pure policy ports;
  43: staging; resource limits; tests. Out: Production strategy change, real exchange,
  44: Production account reads, multi-symbol scheduling, and retirement of
  45: FIX-11K/FIX-11Q/pre-FIX122/EXPERIMENTAL. Those paths remain explicit
  46: `EXPERIMENTAL` and cannot enter baseline output.
  47: 
  48: ## 3. Read/write boundary
  49: 
  50: | Class | Data |
  51: |---|---|
  52: | Forbidden Production account state | `wallet_asset`, `wallet_trade`, `wallet_managed_position`, `paper_position`, `wallet_settings`, account snapshots and daily account statistics. |
  53: | Permitted read-only evidence | `candle`, `trade_signal`, `market_price_event`, `signal_processing_work`, `candle_analysis_outcome`, `order_book_snapshot`, `fix122_evaluation`, `buy_htf_assessment_audit`, `execution_opportunity`, subject to qualifier as-of rules. |
  54: | Replay-owned writes | `regression_test_run`, `analysis_test_signal`, `wallet_execution_test`, `wallet_position_test`, and new RPR6 profile/certificate/staging objects. |
  55: 
  56: `NON_CANONICAL` and `PROTECTION_NOT_APPLIED` observations are deliberate
  57: exclusions. Required accepted observations are `LEGACY`/`APPLIED`, retaining id
  58: and ordering slot.
  59: 
  60: ## 4. Complete class/file registry
  61: 
  62: | ID | Artifact | Change | Production impact |
  63: |---|---|---|---|
  64: | RPR6-01 | `RegressionTestWorker` | qualifier before staging; closed identity timeline | none |
  65: | RPR6-02 | `ReplayEvidenceQualifier` new | certificate/mode/refusal | none |
  66: | RPR6-03 | profile/store new | immutable virtual account/config | none |
  67: | RPR6-04 | virtual-account adapter new | balances/positions/effects | none |
  68: | RPR6-05 | `ExecutionReplayScope` | stable run keys, clocks, finally cleanup | shared; no non-Replay behavior change |
  69: | RPR6-06 | `ShadowProductionReplayService` | staging, truthful labels, ordered calls | none |
  70: | RPR6-07 | `TradeExecutionValidationService` | Replay settings port | shared; Production regression/latency proof |
  71: | RPR6-08 | `DynamicProfitLockService` | Replay settings port | shared; Production regression/latency proof |
  72: | RPR6-09 | `LivePositionProtectionService`, `PositionManagementService`, `PaperTradingService` | expose pure ordered inputs, no policy reorder | shared; transaction trace/regression |
  73: | RPR6-10 | `PositionExitPolicy`, `ProfitLockPolicy`, `BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy` | virtual constraints, no Replay HTTP | shared; existing Production provider unchanged |
  74: | RPR6-11 | `OneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver` | experimental profile port and label | Replay only |
  75: | RPR6-12 | `RegimeStateService` | guaranteed Replay cleanup | shared; no Production behavior change |
  76: | RPR6-13 | `ReplayParityValidator` | analysis-only oracle | Replay only |
  77: | RPR6-14 | next verified Flyway migration | profile/certificate/staging/trace schema | additive only |
  78: | RPR6-15 | typed replay configuration | explicit modes and resource bounds | runtime protection |
  79: | RPR6-16 | tests | A8 suite | none |
  80: | RPR6-17 | docs | A10 final docs | none |
  81: 
  82: ## 5. Method contract
  83: 
  84: | ID | Area | Required behavior |
  85: |---|---|---|
  86: | M01 | worker start | qualifier replaces the later pending-evidence write guard; it runs before any Replay business write. |
  87: | M02 | timeline/snapshot | require `closed=1` and exact symbol/TF/open/close identity; mismatch refuses before staging. |
  88: | M03 | context | select stored as-of evidence, historical recomputation, or refusal per dependency; never fallback to future data. |
  89: | M04 | staging | no skip semantics. Any mid-run invariant/error discards staged artifacts and records only terminal run failure. |
  90: | M05 | profile port | wallet/account/HTTP access in Replay throws named forbidden-dependency failure before publication. |
  91: | M06 | order | price then signal path: advisory stop, TP without extension, label-free lock, rebase/floor, SELL/add suppression, progressive add. Simulation records price assumptions. |
  92: | M07 | virtual budget | virtual daily-check exception ends `FAILED_PROFILE_EVALUATION`; never consults Production account data. |
  93: | M08 | scope | clear Replay and `RegimeStateService` state in `finally` for success, cancel and error. |
  94: 
  95: ## 6. Database/configuration/logging
  96: 
  97: | ID | Item | Requirement |
  98: |---|---|---|
  99: | D01 | migration | allocate next free migration only after read-only Flyway-history preflight; never edit V103/V106ƒ?"V109. |
 100: | D02 | MySQL validation | prove baseline/upgrade; V103 collector dependency must be explicit, never silently satisfied. |
 101: | C01 | `replay.default-mode` | `PRODUCTION_LOGIC_SIMULATION`. |
 102: | C02 | `replay.max-concurrent-runs` | `1`. |
 103: | C03 | `replay.profile.required` | `true`; no wallet-settings fallback. |
 104: | C04 | executor/pool limit | bounded; reject before harming Production freshness. |
 105: | L01 | INFO `REPLAY_QUALIFIED` | run/mode/baseline/config/evidence counts. |
 106: | L02 | INFO `REPLAY_REFUSED` | run/requested mode/reason codes. |
 107: | L03 | DEBUG `REPLAY_DECISION` | run/key/route/result/evidence IDs/order/clocks. |
 108: | L04 | WARN `REPLAY_FORBIDDEN_DEPENDENCY` | component/dependency type/run, no secret. |
 109: | L05 | INFO `REPLAY_COMPLETED` | earned mode/rows/duration/resources. |
 110: 
 111: ## 7. Trading behavior
 112: 
 113: Production policy is unchanged. Replayƒ?Ts entry/exit/add/TP/lock/stop behavior
 114: changes only by calling the same extracted pure policies with virtual account
 115: data. Fees/slippage are profile data and zero-fee remains explicitly labelled
 116: when selected. Historical-only/skipped work is reproduced from durable outcome
 117: evidence or analysis parity is refused. Same-boundary higher-timeframe
 118: visibility requires recorded/inferred ordering; otherwise strict analysis claim
 119: is refused.
 120: 
 121: ## 8. Tests and validation
 122: 
 123: | ID | Scenario | Expected result |
 124: |---|---|---|
 125: | X01 | open/identity-mismatched candle | refusal before output |
 126: | X02 | missing context/config/lineage | analysis claim refused; simulation only with declared inputs |
 127: | X03 | excluded price event status | no false evidence failure |
 128: | X04 | account or HTTP port | fail before publication |
 129: | X05 | Replay signal key | stable and never equal to Production ID; near-TP/consumption works |
 130: | X06 | Production policy fixtures | exact ordered stop/TP/lock/floor/SELL/add outcomes |
 131: | X07 | virtual budget exception | named failed run, no account read |
 132: | X08 | experimental observer | baseline never invokes; output labelled experimental |
 133: | X09 | cancellation/error | state cleanup and staged artifact discard |
 134: | X10 | concurrent Replay/load | one-run/pool bounds preserve freshness |
 135: | X11 | real MySQL migration | next migration, B105/V103 policy and upgrade path proven |
 136: | X12 | baseline hash | CI rejects mismatched source/config request |
 137: 
 138: ## 9. Risks and rollback
 139: 
 140: Risks: policy drift through shared extraction, latency, look-ahead, Flyway
 141: collision and false labels. Mitigate with pure ports, Production regression and
 142: transaction tests, qualification refusal, preflight and mode metrics. Rollback
 143: disables the new Replay route; it never falls back to wallet reads. Schema is
 144: additive and corrected only forward.
 145: 
 146: ## 10. Documentation and reconciliation
 147: 
 148: After approved implementation and validation only, update relevant
 149: `codex-docs` and `claude-docs` architecture/changelog/trading/investigation
 150: documents. Shared knowledge/decisions change only through dual agreement.
 151: Implementation report must map every RPR6/M/D/C/L/X item and every actual file
 152: to this registry; any missing or unregistered material work rejects acceptance.
 153: 
 154: ## 11. Approval
 155: 
 156: Codex: NOT REVIEWED  
 157: Claude: NOT REVIEWED  
 158: Unresolved disagreements: NOT REVIEWED  
 159: User authorization: NOT PROVIDED
 160: 
 161: No implementation starts until this exact revision is dual-approved, frozen to
 162: the required PDF artifact, and explicitly approved by the user.

===== agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md =====
   1: # Replay / Production Business-Logic-Parity Redesign
   2: 
   3: **Revision:** RPR-0.4-DRAFT  
   4: **Status:** DRAFT ƒ?" NOT APPROVED ƒ?" IMPLEMENTATION FORBIDDEN  
   5: **Created:** 2026-10-10  
   6: **Scope owner:** Codex implementation / Claude independent review
   7: 
   8: ## 1. Business objective
   9: 
  10: Make manual Replay reproduce the current paper-trading Production business flow
  11: as closely as durable historical evidence permits, while remaining completely
  12: isolated from Production trading state and wallet data.
  13: 
  14: The exact intended flow is:
  15: 
  16: ```text
  17: historical candle + historical context + canonical accepted price events
  18:   -> shared Production indicator/signal/decision policies
  19:   -> replay-local virtual wallet/account state
  20:   -> shared sizing/allocation/entry revalidation policies
  21:   -> replay-local position lifecycle
  22:   -> shared TP/SL/profit-lock/continuation/exit policies
  23:   -> replay output + explicit parity certificate
  24: ```
  25: 
  26: Replay must never write `wallet_*`, `paper_position`, `trade_signal`,
  27: `signal_processing_work`, or any other Production trading table.
  28: 
  29: The result is not a claim that historical Java scheduling, MySQL lock timing,
  30: network delay, external Binance availability, or the historical Production
  31: wallet balance can be reproduced. It is a verified **business-logic parity**
  32: claim only when all required market/decision evidence exists. Otherwise Replay
  33: remains available as a labelled simulation and may not claim parity.
  34: 
  35: ## 2. Confirmed root cause and evidence
  36: 
  37: The 2026-10-10 source audit established the following:
  38: 
  39: 1. Signal, entry, normal-exit, continuation, profit-lock, and near-TP policy
  40:    use several shared Production services.
  41: 2. `ShadowProductionReplayService` separately owns replay wallet cash,
  42:    positions, daily budget, persistence, price-loop lifecycle, and result
  43:    labels. It starts at `INITIAL_CAPITAL=10000`, runs one symbol/position, and
  44:    does not reproduce Production wallet/global controls.
  45: 3. A run is labelled `EXACT_PRICE_REPLAY` when at least one accepted price
  46:    event exists, rather than proving coverage for every interval while a
  47:    position was open.
  48: 4. Historical derivatives information is not persisted;
  49:    `DerivativesPositioningService.evaluateHistorical()` returns UNAVAILABLE.
  50:    Historical order-book evaluation can likewise be unavailable when snapshots
  51:    were not retained.
  52: 5. Replay-only FIX-11K and FIX-11Q observers, and the pre-FIX-122 UI revision,
  53:    have no current Production equivalent and must not contaminate parity runs.
  54: 6. Current Replay reads `WalletSettingsRepository` directly and its near-TP
  55:    path can call `BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy`, which can request a current
  56:    live Binance minimum notional. Both violate replay account/external-input
  57:    isolation.
  58: 
  59: ## 3. Boundaries
  60: 
  61: ### In scope
  62: 
  63: - Replay input/evidence qualification and run-level parity status.
  64: - Replay-local virtual wallet state, wallet controls, positions, executions,
  65:   daily statistics, and global limit reproduction.
  66: - Canonical price-event coverage qualification and event ordering.
  67: - Historical context-evidence qualification for derivatives and order book.
  68: - Removal of approved retired Replay-only business/experiment behaviour.
  69: - Parity certificate, diagnostics, migrations, tests, documentation.
  70: 
  71: ### Out of scope
  72: 
  73: - Any Production strategy change, sizing change, exit change, or wallet change.
  74: - Live-exchange orders, fills, fees, slippage, or order-book execution modelling.
  75: - Replaying database locks, thread scheduling, JVM pauses, WebSocket transport,
  76:   external REST failures, or transaction retries as historical facts.
  77: - FIX-153, FIX-154, and FIX-155 trading-policy changes.
  78: - Deleting Production evidence or existing historical Replay/archive rows.
  79: 
  80: ## 4. Contractual modes
  81: 
  82: | Mode | Purpose | Missing evidence behaviour | Claim allowed |
  83: |---|---|---|---|
  84: | `BUSINESS_LOGIC_PARITY` | Reproduce Production rules using an explicitly selected virtual Replay account and complete market/decision evidence. | Refuse the run before execution. | Business-logic parity. |
  85: | `SIMULATION` | Manual what-if/research run. | Continue but persist all approximation reasons. | Simulation only. |
  86: | `EXPERIMENTAL_RESEARCH` | Explicit, separately approved research such as FIX-155. | Per experiment contract. | Never Production parity. |
  87: 
  88: The default Administration Replay action must be `BUSINESS_LOGIC_PARITY`; the UI
  89: must make `SIMULATION` and `EXPERIMENTAL_RESEARCH` explicit opt-in choices.
  90: 
  91: ## 5. Business-logic-parity eligibility
  92: 
  93: A `BUSINESS_LOGIC_PARITY` run must fail before generated signals or shadow wallet
  94: state are written unless all of the following hold:
  95: 
  96: 1. Every requested candle identity is closed and within the quality contract.
  97: 2. A complete immutable `ReplayAccountProfile` is supplied: starting virtual
  98:    USDT, reserve, daily budget, daily loss state, maximum-new-position/global
  99:    limits, and wallet settings. It is copied to replay-only tables at run start
 100:    and is never read from or written to a Production wallet table.
 101: 3. The selected replay universe is declared. The virtual account applies
 102:    global limits across every symbol included in that run; no claim is made
 103:    that it reproduces the historical Production wallet's other positions.
 104: 4. The selected profile contains versioned execution constraints for relevant
 105:    symbols, including minimum executable notional. Replay never fetches a
 106:    current exchange constraint while evaluating historical events.
 107: 5. Every canonical direct price observation required from the first replay
 108:    position open through its terminal close is represented by accepted
 109:    `LEGACY`/`APPLIED` evidence, with no unresolved `PENDING` row in the whole
 110:    consumed range.
 111: 6. Required historical order-book evidence exists whenever the feature was
 112:    enabled for the corresponding Production decision.
 113: 7. Required historical derivatives evidence exists whenever derivatives were
 114:    enabled and could change confidence or entry authority. Until durable
 115:    derivatives persistence exists, affected requests must be refused in this
 116:    mode rather than silently treating UNAVAILABLE as equivalent.
 117: 8. The exact Production configuration/version fingerprint is available for the
 118:    requested period, or all version-dependent decision paths are marked
 119:    unavailable and the mode is refused.
 120: 
 121: `SIMULATION` may use configured defaults or fixed starting capital but must
 122: store each deviation in its output. It may never be displayed as parity.
 123: 
 124: ### 5.1 Wallet isolation boundary
 125: 
 126: Replay never reads Production `wallet_asset`, `wallet_settings`,
 127: `wallet_trade`, `wallet_managed_position`, `paper_position`, daily-statistics,
 128: or wallet-snapshot rows as a decision input. The existing data does not retain
 129: complete historical wallet state and is deliberately irrelevant to a Replay
 130: account. A user-selected/versioned `ReplayAccountProfile` is the only account
 131: input. This preserves safe isolation while exercising the same pure sizing and
 132: position-management formulas.
 133: 
 134: The profile also contains symbol execution constraints used by shared
 135: paper-order guards. `BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy` must be split into a pure
 136: minimum-notional evaluator plus a Production-only exchange-constraint provider;
 137: Replay receives immutable profile values and may not issue Binance HTTP.
 138: 
 139: ## 6. Agreed behaviour changes
 140: 
 141: ### 6.1 Replay starting state
 142: 
 143: Replace hard-coded starting capital with an immutable `ReplayStartingState`.
 144: It is seeded from the selected, versioned `ReplayAccountProfile` and stored
 145: under the replay run ID before processing begins. Shadow wallet/position
 146: services consume only this replay-local state and replay tables.
 147: 
 148: The run-start state must include profile ID/version, creation timestamp, version/config fingerprint,
 149: virtual USDT/asset balances, daily-budget state, daily loss state, virtual
 150: managed positions, virtual paper positions, replay idempotency keys, and a
 151: versioned wallet/settings/configuration fingerprint. It may not be refreshed
 152: from Production while a run is executing.
 153: 
 154: ### 6.2 Shared paper-wallet business authority
 155: 
 156: Extract or complete pure shared policies for:
 157: 
 158: - initial and progressive sizing;
 159: - reserve, daily budget, daily-loss, maximum-new-position, and global
 160:   maximum-open-position controls;
 161: - buy/sell/partial-harvest quantity and cost-basis calculations;
 162: - position average entry, target/stop updates, profit-lock state, and near-TP
 163:   state transitions;
 164: - idempotency-key interpretation.
 165: 
 166: Production retains its current transactional repositories. Replay receives
 167: replay-local adapters implementing the same policy inputs/outputs. No policy
 168: may branch on `replay=true` to produce a different business decision.
 169: 
 170: ### 6.3 Price evidence
 171: 
 172: Replace the boolean/label derived from ƒ?oany event existsƒ?? with a coverage
 173: result containing requested interval, covered interval, gaps, accepted event
 174: count, rejected/unresolved count, and every position-open interval requiring
 175: protection.
 176: 
 177: `BUSINESS_LOGIC_PARITY` rejects incomplete coverage. `SIMULATION` may use
 178: signal-price fallback only with the persisted reason `PRICE_PATH_INCOMPLETE`.
 179: 
 180: ### 6.4 Historical decision evidence
 181: 
 182: Persist versioned derivatives and order-book observations required by
 183: Production, or mark affected historical intervals ineligible for business-logic
 184: parity. Replay must use the same normalized evaluator and only evidence at or
 185: before the replay clock. It must never call live Binance endpoints.
 186: 
 187: ### 6.5 Event ordering and time
 188: 
 189: Use canonical UTC `observed_at` and event IDs for price ordering. Define a
 190: single deterministic tie order matching Productionƒ?Ts committed sequence:
 191: 
 192: 1. accepted price protection event;
 193: 2. resulting position/wallet state;
 194: 3. closed-candle analysis signal;
 195: 4. signal-driven position action.
 196: 
 197: If durable evidence cannot establish the required tie order, business-logic parity
 198: must refuse the run. Replay timestamps remain presentation-neutral UTC;
 199: KSA conversion belongs only to UI/reporting.
 200: 
 201: ### 6.6 Retired Replay-only behaviour
 202: 
 203: The following are **candidates for removal**, subject to complete-reference
 204: review and approval. They must be removed from baseline parity runs now; any
 205: future research requires an independently approved module and run mode.
 206: 
 207: | Registry ID | Candidate | Proposed disposition |
 208: |---|---|---|
 209: | RPR-RM-01 | `DefensiveRiskReductionReplayObserver` / FIX-11K | Remove source, controller/UI/output tables only after archive/export compatibility is proved. |
 210: | RPR-RM-02 | `OneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver` / FIX-11Q | Remove source, controller/UI/output tables only after archive/export compatibility is proved. |
 211: | RPR-RM-03 | pre-FIX-122 replay revision selector | Remove the UI/API option and old comparison path; Production-current FIX-122 remains the only parity rule. |
 212: | RPR-RM-04 | `EXPERIMENTAL` logic mode if no approved user-facing experiment consumes it | Remove after exact reference search; otherwise retain as an isolated framework with no baseline access. |
 213: 
 214: `ReplayDataSource.DATABASE` versus `DATASET` is not retired business logic:
 215: both must remain only if a test proves equal outputs. It is a data-access
 216: implementation choice, not an old Production-rule selector.
 217: 
 218: ## 7. Implementation change registry
 219: 
 220: No implementation item is authorized until this exact revision is approved.
 221: 
 222: | ID | Area | Required change |
 223: |---|---|---|
 224: | RPR-01 | run contract | Add persisted mode, evidence status, account profile/state ID, version/config fingerprint, and refusal reason to the replay run. |
 225: | RPR-02 | replay account schema/service | Add `ReplayAccountProfile` and immutable run-start-state tables. Seed virtual state only from the selected Replay profile. |
 226: | RPR-03 | wallet authority | Extract/reuse pure paper-wallet policy and provide Production/replay persistence adapters; remove direct Replay reads of `WalletSettingsRepository`. |
 227: | RPR-03a | execution constraints | Split minimum-notional evaluation from live exchange constraint acquisition; inject immutable profile constraints into Replay and prohibit Replay Binance HTTP. |
 228: | RPR-04 | shadow replay | Replace fixed capital/local single-position assumptions with replay-local state and multi-symbol/global-limit coordinator. |
 229: | RPR-05 | price evidence | Add interval coverage validator and replace the current any-event exact-price label. |
 230: | RPR-06 | historical context | Add versioned persistence/lookup/qualification for order book and derivatives, or explicit parity refusal. |
 231: | RPR-07 | timing/order | Persist/use deterministic committed event ordering and reject unknown ordering for parity runs. |
 232: | RPR-08 | certificate | Add immutable parity certificate containing field comparisons, evidence counts, deviations, and final claim. |
 233: | RPR-09 | removals | Execute only the approved RPR-RM entries, including migration/archive compatibility. |
 234: | RPR-10 | UI/API | Make mode explicit; default to business-logic parity; display refusal/deviation/certificate details. |
 235: | RPR-11 | tests | Add unit, integration, MySQL, complete scenario, no-look-ahead, and Production/Replay comparison tests below. |
 236: | RPR-12 | docs | Update architecture, rules, changelog, investigation, operational runbook, and parity contract. |
 237: 
 238: ## 8. Error handling, concurrency, and transactions
 239: 
 240: - Replay-state creation is isolated and must be consistent for one profile version.
 241: - Business-logic-parity evidence validation happens before replay execution; no
 242:   partial shadow run may be labelled a parity run.
 243: - Replay output writes are transactionally isolated by `test_run_id`; a failed
 244:   run records its failure and certificate status without changing Production.
 245: - Replay must not lock Production wallet/position rows for business mutation.
 246: - Replay-profile/state reads must not expose credentials and must log only IDs,
 247:   UTC times, symbol counts, mode, and evidence result.
 248: - Cancellation preserves isolated run evidence and cannot leave a completed
 249:   parity certificate.
 250: 
 251: ## 9. Logging and observability registry
 252: 
 253: | ID | Event | Mandatory fields |
 254: |---|---|---|
 255: | RPR-L-01 | virtual starting state created/refused | runId, mode, profile ID/version, virtual balances/position counts, config fingerprint, reason |
 256: | RPR-L-02 | evidence qualified/refused | runId, symbol(s), from/to UTC, candle/price/orderbook/derivatives status, gap counts |
 257: | RPR-L-03 | shadow execution decision | runId, symbol, signal/candle ID, decision code, shared-policy source, replay state ID |
 258: | RPR-L-04 | parity mismatch | runId, field, Production value, replay value, evidence reference, severity |
 259: | RPR-L-05 | certificate completed | runId, claim, matched/mismatched field counts, simulation deviations, duration |
 260: | RPR-L-06 | retired path blocked | runId, removed/experimental feature ID, requested source, outcome |
 261: 
 262: ## 10. Database/configuration/trading impact
 263: 
 264: - **Database:** additive replay account/snapshot/certificate/evidence schema and any
 265:   required durable historical derivatives observations. Applied migrations are
 266:   never edited. Archive migration/retention is mandatory before deletion of
 267:   retired Replay-only output tables.
 268: - **Configuration:** add replay mode/evidence policy only. Production defaults
 269:   and trading configuration remain unchanged.
 270: - **Trading behaviour:** NONE. Production is read-only to this feature and no
 271:   live/paper Production decision, wallet mutation, position, or protection
 272:   rule may change.
 273: - **Replay behaviour:** baseline becomes evidence-qualified business-logic parity
 274:   or explicitly labelled simulation; retired experimental paths are removed or
 275:   isolated only as approved above.
 276: 
 277: ## 11. Test and validation registry
 278: 
 279: | ID | Required proof |
 280: |---|---|
 281: | RPR-T-01 | The same profile inputs produce equal Production/replay sizing, reserve, daily budget, daily-loss, and global-position-limit decisions through shared policies. |
 282: | RPR-T-01a | Replay refuses `BUSINESS_LOGIC_PARITY` when no immutable replay account profile is selected; it must never silently use a Production wallet row. |
 283: | RPR-T-02 | A missing, unresolved, rejected, or coverage-gapped price event refuses business-logic parity and labels simulation correctly. |
 284: | RPR-T-03 | Price-event/signal same-time ordering matches Production for TP, SL, profit lock, normal exit, and signal SELL. |
 285: | RPR-T-04 | End-to-end initial BUY, progressive add, target extension, rebase, stop, profit lock, near-TP partial harvest, terminal exit, and P&L lifecycle match field-for-field. |
 286: | RPR-T-05 | Historical order book/derivatives use only as-of persisted data; missing evidence refuses parity; no live HTTP occurs. |
 287: | RPR-T-05a | Replay never reads a Production wallet settings row or calls a Binance minimum-notional endpoint; profile values drive the same pure minimum-order decision. |
 288: | RPR-T-06 | Multi-symbol global wallet/max-position test proves no isolated-symbol bypass. |
 289: | RPR-T-07 | Cancellation/error tests prove Production tables remain unchanged and certificates cannot falsely pass. |
 290: | RPR-T-08 | MySQL migration and transaction tests use a disposable database. |
 291: | RPR-T-09 | Dataset and database replay inputs produce equivalent output for the same qualified evidence, or one path is removed. |
 292: | RPR-T-10 | Removal tests prove baseline Replay cannot invoke FIX-11K, FIX-11Q, or pre-FIX-122 paths. |
 293: | RPR-T-11 | Real historical parity suite compares selected known Production windows and produces stored certificates. |
 294: 
 295: ## 12. Risks, rollback, and acceptance
 296: 
 297: - Risk: historical data required for verified parity may not exist. Mitigation:
 298:   refuse parity and use clearly labelled simulation; do not invent evidence.
 299: - Risk: snapshot schema increases storage. Mitigation: bounded retention and
 300:   archive strategy, never deletion of active/investigation evidence.
 301: - Risk: retiring observers breaks historical UI/archive reads. Mitigation:
 302:   migration and archive/export compatibility tests before removal.
 303: - Rollback: disable new business-logic-parity mode and retain existing archived
 304:   output; Production requires no rollback because it is never mutated.
 305: 
 306: Acceptance requires: all registry IDs reconciled; no unregistered code change;
 307: all test evidence green; disposable MySQL validation; complete source review by
 308: Claude; Codex verification of the exact reviewed revision; and explicit user
 309: authorization after both agents approve this same document.
 310:

===== agent-docs/PDF_ARTIFACT_RULES.md =====
   1: # Frozen PDF Artifact Rules
   2: 
   3: Markdown remains the machine-readable source. A PDF is the frozen human-review artifact for major approval gates.
   4: 
   5: Mandatory frozen artifacts when the corresponding stage exists:
   6: - `FINAL_TECHNICAL_REVIEW-Rxx.pdf`
   7: - `FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION-Rxx.pdf`
   8: - `IMPLEMENTATION_REPORT-Rxx.pdf`
   9: - `VALIDATION_REPORT-Rxx.pdf`
  10: 
  11: Each frozen PDF must include the source revision, SHA-256 of the exact source Markdown, Codex verdict, Claude verdict, implementation authorization state, and a statement that any material source change invalidates the frozen revision.
  12: 
  13: Use `scripts/freeze-agent-artifact.ps1`. A dual-approved specification is not READY FOR USER REVIEW until its PDF freeze succeeds. PDF generation never grants implementation authorization.

  85:     public boolean requestStop(long runId) {
  86:         if (!activeRunIds.contains(runId)) return false;
  87:         stopRequestedRunIds.add(runId);
  88:         return true;
  89:     }
  90: 
  91:     public boolean isStopRequested(long runId) {
  92:         return stopRequestedRunIds.contains(runId);
  93:     }

  95:     private void checkStopRequested(long runId) {
  96:         if (stopRequestedRunIds.contains(runId)) throw new ReplayCancellationException(runId);
  97:     }

  99:     @Async
 100:     public void runAsync(long runId, ReplayDataSource dataSource) {
 101:         // FIX-087: close the race at worker entry as well. Even if two callers schedule the same
 102:         // run almost simultaneously, only one may execute the deterministic replay pipeline.
 103:         if (!activeRunIds.add(runId)) {
 104:             log.warn("FIX-087: replay run {} already has an active worker; duplicate execution skipped", runId);
 105:             return;
 106:         }
 107:         // FIX-11J: Replay-only observational timers. The worker pipeline below is intentionally
 108:         // left in its existing order; these locals only capture elapsed monotonic time and are
 109:         // persisted once in finally so diagnostics cannot steer or fail Replay business logic.
 110:         final long replayTotalStartedNs = System.nanoTime();

 112:         Long verifyEventResolutionNs = null;

 118:             long stageStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 119:             Map<String, Object> run = jdbcTemplate.queryForMap(
 120:                     "SELECT symbol, start_time, end_time FROM analysis_test_run WHERE id = ?", runId);
 121:             String symbol = String.valueOf(run.get("symbol"));

 123:             Instant end = toInstant(run.get("end_time"));

 130:             List<Candle> fiveMinuteCandles = candles(symbol, "5m", contextStart, end);

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestWorker.java lines 535-700 =====
 535:     private FreshReplayStats generateFreshSignals(
 536:             long runId,
 537:             String symbol,
 538:             Instant executionStart,
 539:             Instant executionEnd,
 540:             List<Candle> oneMinuteCandles,
 541:             List<Candle> fiveMinuteCandles,
 542:             List<Candle> oneHourCandles,
 543:             String btcSymbol,
 544:             List<Candle> btcOneMinuteCandles,
 545:             List<Candle> btcFiveMinuteCandles,
 546:             List<Candle> btcOneHourCandles,
 547:             ReplayDataSource dataSource,
 548:             ReplayDataset replayDataset
 549:     ) {
 550:         List<ReplayCandle> timeline = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 551:         oneMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "1m", c, true)));
 552:         fiveMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "5m", c, true)));
 553:         oneHourCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "1h", c, true)));
 554:         btcOneMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "1m", c, false)));
 555:         btcFiveMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "5m", c, false)));
 556:         btcOneHourCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "1h", c, false)));
 557:         timeline.sort(java.util.Comparator
 558:                 .comparing((ReplayCandle rc) -> rc.candle().getOpenTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(rc.interval())))
 559:                 .thenComparingInt(rc -> intervalOrder(rc.interval()))
 560:                 .thenComparingInt(rc -> rc.primary() ? 1 : 0));
 561: 
 562:         int oneMinuteSignals = 0, fiveMinuteSignals = 0, oneHourSignals = 0;
 563:         int oneMinuteBuys = 0, fiveMinuteBuys = 0, oneHourBuys = 0;
 564:         int buys = 0, watches = 0, sells = 0, strongSells = 0, errors = 0;
 565:         java.util.List<TradeSignal> generatedSignals = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 566: 
 567:         // FIX-11L: Replay-only deep profiling for the Generate fresh signals stage.
 568:         // These monotonic timers observe existing calls only; they do not alter inputs,
 569:         // call ordering, persistence, thresholds, or any Production trading behavior.
 570:         ReplayFreshSignalProfiler profiler = new ReplayFreshSignalProfiler(runId, dataSource);
 571:         // FIX-11M: Enable aggregate timing only for this Replay worker thread.
 572:         // The shared AnalysisService decision path is unchanged; this is observation-only.
 573:         analysisService.beginReplayAnalysisProfiling(runId);
 574:         try (ExecutionReplayScope.Scope ignored = replayScope.open(runId, List.of(), o -> {})) {
 575:         // FIX-043 Replay parity: fresh Replay has always generated technical snapshots/signals
 576:         // chronologically for EVERY closed candle in the timeline. Production now restores the
 577:         // same no-gap contract with asynchronous close-event processing plus chronological recovery.
 578:         // Do not "optimize" Replay by sampling every Nth candle or by copying the production recovery
 579:         // scheduler cadence; that would recreate the exact 1m->~5m blind-gap incident FIX-043 fixes.
 580:         for (int index = 0; index < timeline.size(); index++) {
 581:             checkStopRequested(runId);
 582:             ReplayCandle replay = timeline.get(index);
 583:             Candle candle = replay.candle();
 584:             Instant evaluationTime = candle.getOpenTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(replay.interval()));
 585:             boolean inRequestedWindow = !evaluationTime.isBefore(executionStart) && !evaluationTime.isAfter(executionEnd);
 586:             try {
 587:                 long operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 588:                 java.util.Optional<IndicatorSnapshot> snapshot;
 589:                 try {
 590:                     snapshot = dataSource == ReplayDataSource.DATASET
 591:                             ? technicalIndicatorService.calculateSnapshotForRegression(
 592:                                     replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle.getOpenTime(), replayDataset)
 593:                             : technicalIndicatorService.calculateSnapshotForRegression(
 594:                                     replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle.getOpenTime());
 595:                 } finally {
 596:                     profiler.recordSnapshot(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
 597:                 }
 598:                 if (snapshot.isEmpty()) {
 599:                     if (inRequestedWindow) {
 600:                         errors++;
 601:                         saveGenerationError(runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle,
 602:                                 "Not enough historical candles to calculate the technical snapshot");
 603:                     }
 604:                     continue;
 605:                 }
 606: 
 607:                 // Do not generate a signal whose candle would only have closed after the
 608:                 // requested test end. Warm-up rows before executionStart are intentionally
 609:                 // generated for as-of context, but are not counted or displayed as test output.
 610:                 if (evaluationTime.isAfter(executionEnd)) continue;
 611: 
 612:                 replayScope.reference(evaluationTime);
 613:                 operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 614:                 TradeSignal generated;
 615:                 try {
 616:                     generated = analysisService.analyzeForRegression(snapshot.get(), evaluationTime);
 617:                 } finally {
 618:                     profiler.recordAnalysis(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
 619:                 }
 620:                 replayScope.appendSignal(generated);
 621:                 SignalDecision decision = generated.getDecision();
 622:                 if (replay.primary()) generatedSignals.add(generated);
 623: 
 624:                 if (replay.primary() && inRequestedWindow) {
 625:                     if ("1m".equals(replay.interval())) { oneMinuteSignals++; if (isBuy(decision)) oneMinuteBuys++; }
 626:                     else if ("5m".equals(replay.interval())) { fiveMinuteSignals++; if (isBuy(decision)) fiveMinuteBuys++; }
 627:                     else if ("1h".equals(replay.interval())) { oneHourSignals++; if (isBuy(decision)) oneHourBuys++; }
 628: 
 629:                     if (decision == SignalDecision.BUY || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY) buys++;
 630:                     else if (decision == SignalDecision.WATCH) watches++;
 631:                     else if (decision == SignalDecision.SELL) sells++;

   9: 
  10: /**
  11:  * FIX-091 / Fix 6: field-level pre-wallet parity comparator.
  12:  * It deliberately compares trading-intelligence state only; wallet execution is outside scope.
  13:  */
  14: @Service
  15: public class ReplayParityValidator {
  16: 
  17:     public Result compare(TradeSignal production, TradeSignal replay) {
  18:         List<String> mismatches = new ArrayList<>();
  19:         if (production == null || replay == null) {
  20:             mismatches.add("Production and Replay signals must both exist");
  21:             return new Result(false, mismatches);
  22:         }
  23: 
  24:         same(mismatches, "detectedRegime", production.getDetectedRegime(), replay.getDetectedRegime());
  25:         same(mismatches, "candidateRegime", production.getCandidateRegime(), replay.getCandidateRegime());
  26:         same(mismatches, "confirmedRegime", production.getConfirmedRegime(), replay.getConfirmedRegime());
  27:         same(mismatches, "selectedStrategy", production.getSelectedStrategy(), replay.getSelectedStrategy());
  28:         same(mismatches, "originalDecision", production.getOriginalDecision(), replay.getOriginalDecision());
  29:         same(mismatches, "finalDecision", production.getDecision(), replay.getDecision());
  30:         same(mismatches, "rawConfidence", production.getRawConfidenceScore(), replay.getRawConfidenceScore());
  31:         same(mismatches, "effectiveConfidence", production.getEffectiveConfidenceScore(), replay.getEffectiveConfidenceScore());
  32:         same(mismatches, "primaryBlockingStage", production.getPrimaryBlockingStage(), replay.getPrimaryBlockingStage());
  33:         same(mismatches, "btcContextStatus", production.getBtcContextStatus(), replay.getBtcContextStatus());
  34:         same(mismatches, "liquidityStatus", production.getLiquidityStatus(), replay.getLiquidityStatus());
  35:         same(mismatches, "entryAuthority", production.getEntryAuthority(), replay.getEntryAuthority());
  36:         same(mismatches, "entryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent",
  37:                 production.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent(), replay.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent());
  38:         return new Result(mismatches.isEmpty(), List.copyOf(mismatches));
  39:     }
  40: 
  41:     private void same(List<String> mismatches, String field, Object expected, Object actual) {
  42:         if (!Objects.equals(expected, actual)) {
  43:             mismatches.add(field + ": Production=" + expected + ", Replay=" + actual);
  44:         }
  45:     }
  46: 
  47:     public record Result(boolean matches, List<String> mismatches) {}
  48: }

 129:     /**
 130:      * FIX-11H REPLAY/PARITY INFRASTRUCTURE ONLY. Production calculateAndPersist() never calls
 131:      * this overload. The only difference is where the already-closed candle list comes from;
 132:      * calculateSnapshot() below remains the exact shared indicator math.
 133:      */
 134:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
 135:     public Optional<IndicatorSnapshot> calculateSnapshotForRegression(
 136:             String symbol, String intervalCode, Instant candleOpenTime, ReplayDataset dataset) {
 137:         if (candleOpenTime == null) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Regression candle open time is required");
 138:         String normalisedSymbol = normaliseSymbol(symbol);
 139:         String normalisedInterval = normaliseInterval(intervalCode);
 140:         List<Candle> candles = new ArrayList<>(
 141:                 dataset.closedCandlesAtOrBefore(normalisedSymbol, normalisedInterval, candleOpenTime, HISTORY_LIMIT));
 142:         if (candles.size() < MINIMUM_CANDLES) return Optional.empty();
 143:         Collections.reverse(candles);
 144:         return Optional.of(calculateSnapshot(normalisedSymbol, normalisedInterval, candles));
 145:     }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/wallet/service/BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.java lines 1-105 =====
   1: package com.crypto.wallet.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.client.binance.BinanceMarketDataClient;
   4: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
   5: import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
   6: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   7: 
   8: import java.math.BigDecimal;
   9: import java.math.MathContext;
  10: import java.util.Locale;
  11: 
  12: /**
  13:  * FIX-11T minimum-executable-order guard shared by Production Paper and Replay.
  14:  *
  15:  * The project intentionally models Paper/Replay as real Binance trading with only the
  16:  * final connector missing.  Therefore a Near-TP partial harvest that Binance would reject
  17:  * for being below the symbol's minimum notional is not simulated.  No alternative exit
  18:  * size is invented: the position simply continues through its existing management logic.
  19:  *
  20:  * This class deliberately checks only the agreed minimum executable notional boundary.
  21:  * It does not add a new Near-TP-specific step-size/precision model.
  22:  */
  23: @Component
  24: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  25: @Slf4j
  26: public class BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy {
  27:     private static final MathContext MC = MathContext.DECIMAL64;
  28: 
  29:     private final BinanceMarketDataClient binanceMarketDataClient;
  30:     public Evaluation evaluate(String symbol, BigDecimal quantity, BigDecimal price) {
  31:         if (symbol == null || symbol.isBlank() || quantity == null || quantity.signum() <= 0
  32:                 || price == null || price.signum() <= 0) {
  33:             return new Evaluation(false, "INVALID_ORDER_INPUT", BigDecimal.ZERO, null);
  34:         }
  35:         String pair = symbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  36:         BigDecimal notional = quantity.multiply(price, MC);
  37:         BigDecimal minimum;
  38:         try {
  39:             minimum = binanceMarketDataClient.getMinimumExecutableNotional(pair);
  40:         } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
  41:             // Fail safe. Missing exchange constraints must never be converted into permission
  42:             // to create an order that real Binance might reject.
  43:             log.warn("FIX-11T Binance minimum executable amount unavailable: symbol={}, requestedNotional={}, error={}",
  44:                     pair, notional, ex.getMessage());
  45:             return new Evaluation(false, "BINANCE_MINIMUM_UNAVAILABLE", notional, null);
  46:         }
  47:         if (notional.compareTo(minimum) < 0) {
  48:             return new Evaluation(false, "BELOW_BINANCE_MINIMUM", notional, minimum);
  49:         }
  50:         return new Evaluation(true, "EXECUTABLE", notional, minimum);
  51:     }
  52: 
  53:     public record Evaluation(boolean executable, String code, BigDecimal requestedNotional, BigDecimal minimumNotional) {
  54:     }
  55: }

 167:     private Aggregation aggregate(String symbol, Instant reference) {
 168:         Instant now = reference == null ? Instant.now() : reference;
 169:         Instant after = now.minus(properties.activeWindow());
 170:         List<SentimentSignal> signals = repository
 171:                 .findBySymbolAndObservedAtAfterAndObservedAtLessThanEqualOrderByObservedAtDesc(
 172:                         symbol, after, now);
 173: 
 174:         Map<String, List<SentimentSignal>> byProvider = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 175:         for (SentimentSignal signal : signals) {
 176:             String provider = SentimentProviderName.normalize(signal.getSource());
 177:             byProvider.computeIfAbsent(provider, ignored -> new ArrayList<>()).add(signal);
 178:         }
 179: 
 180:         for (com.crypto.domain.SentimentProviderConfig configured : providerConfigService.findAll()) {
 181:             byProvider.computeIfAbsent(configured.getProviderCode(), ignored -> new ArrayList<>());
 182:         }
 183: 
 184:         List<ProviderSentiment> breakdown = new ArrayList<>();
 185:         BigDecimal total = BigDecimal.ZERO;
 186:         BigDecimal denominator = BigDecimal.ZERO;
 187: 
 188:         for (Map.Entry<String, List<SentimentSignal>> entry : byProvider.entrySet()) {
 189:             String providerName = entry.getKey();
 190:             com.crypto.domain.SentimentProviderConfig providerConfig = providerConfigService.require(providerName);
 191:             ProviderAggregate provider = aggregateProvider(entry.getValue(), now);
 192:             BigDecimal configuredWeight = providerConfig.getWeight();
 193:             BigDecimal effectiveWeight = provider.confidence().multiply(configuredWeight, MC);
 194: 
 195:             boolean historicalReference = now.isBefore(Instant.now().minusSeconds(1));
 196:             boolean healthyEnough = historicalReference
 197:                     ? provider.sampleCount() > 0
 198:                     : providerConfigService.contributes(providerConfig, now);
 199:             if (properties.enabled() && providerConfig.isEnabled() && healthyEnough
 200:                     && provider.sampleCount() > 0 && effectiveWeight.signum() > 0) {
 201:                 total = total.add(provider.score().multiply(effectiveWeight, MC), MC);
 202:                 denominator = denominator.add(effectiveWeight, MC);
 203:             }
 204: 
 205:             breakdown.add(new ProviderSentiment(
 206:                     providerName,
 207:                     properties.enabled() && providerConfig.isEnabled(),
 208:                     configuredWeight,
 209:                     provider.score(),
 210:                     provider.confidence(),
 211:                     properties.enabled() && providerConfig.isEnabled() && healthyEnough
 212:                             ? effectiveWeight : BigDecimal.ZERO,
 213:                     provider.sampleCount(),
 214:                     provider.latestObservedAt()
 215:             ));
 216:         }
 217: 
 218:         breakdown.sort(Comparator
 219:                 .comparing(ProviderSentiment::effectiveWeight).reversed()
 220:                 .thenComparing(ProviderSentiment::provider));
 221: 
 222:         BigDecimal score = denominator.signum() == 0
 223:                 ? BigDecimal.ZERO
 224:                 : total.divide(denominator, MC);
 225:         return new Aggregation(score, signals.size(), breakdown);
 226:     }
 227: 
 228:     private ProviderAggregate aggregateProvider(List<SentimentSignal> signals, Instant now) {
 229:         if (signals.isEmpty()) {
 230:             return new ProviderAggregate(BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal.ZERO, 0, null);
 231:         }
 232: 
 233:         BigDecimal weightedTotal = BigDecimal.ZERO;
 234:         BigDecimal totalSampleWeight = BigDecimal.ZERO;
 235:         BigDecimal confidenceTotal = BigDecimal.ZERO;
 236:         Instant latest = null;
 237: 
 238:         for (SentimentSignal signal : signals) {
 239:             long ageMinutes = Math.max(0, Duration.between(signal.getObservedAt(), now).toMinutes());
 240:             BigDecimal timeWeight = BigDecimal.ONE.divide(
 241:                     BigDecimal.ONE.add(BigDecimal.valueOf(ageMinutes / 240.0), MC), MC);
 242:             BigDecimal sampleWeight = signal.getConfidence().multiply(timeWeight, MC);
 243: 
 244:             weightedTotal = weightedTotal.add(signal.getScore().multiply(sampleWeight, MC), MC);
 245:             totalSampleWeight = totalSampleWeight.add(sampleWeight, MC);
 246:             confidenceTotal = confidenceTotal.add(signal.getConfidence().multiply(timeWeight, MC), MC);
 247:             if (latest == null || signal.getObservedAt().isAfter(latest)) latest = signal.getObservedAt();
 248:         }
 249: 
 250:         BigDecimal score = totalSampleWeight.signum() == 0

  18: @Service
  19: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  20: public class SentimentProviderConfigService implements CommandLineRunner {

  22:     private final SentimentProviderConfigRepository repository;
  23:     private final SentimentProperties properties;
  24: 
  25:     private static final Map<String, Seed> SEEDS = new LinkedHashMap<>();
  26:     static {
  27:         SEEDS.put("CRYPTOPANIC", new Seed("CryptoPanic", "CRYPTOPANIC_API_KEY", 300));
  28:         SEEDS.put("NEWS_API", new Seed("NewsAPI", "NEWS_API_KEY", 300));
  29:         SEEDS.put("REDDIT", new Seed("Reddit", "REDDIT_ACCESS_TOKEN", 600));
  30:         SEEDS.put("X", new Seed("X / Twitter", "X_BEARER_TOKEN", 300));
  31:         SEEDS.put("FEAR_GREED", new Seed("Fear & Greed", null, 900));
  32:         SEEDS.put("BINANCE_ANNOUNCEMENT", new Seed("Binance Announcements", null, 300));
  33:         SEEDS.put("WHALE_ALERT", new Seed("Whale Alert", "WHALE_ALERT_API_KEY", 300));
  34:         SEEDS.put("MANUAL_NEWS", new Seed("Manual News", null, 300));
  35:     }
  36: 
  37:     @Override
  38:     @Transactional
  39:     public void run(String... args) {
  40:         for (Map.Entry<String, Seed> entry : SEEDS.entrySet()) {
  41:             String code = entry.getKey();
  42:             if (repository.findByProviderCodeIgnoreCase(code).isPresent()) continue;
  43:             String propertyKey = code.toLowerCase();
  44:             SentimentProperties.Provider configured = properties.provider(propertyKey);
  45:             Seed seed = entry.getValue();
  46:             repository.save(SentimentProviderConfig.builder()
  47:                     .providerCode(code)
  48:                     .displayName(seed.displayName())
  49:                     .enabled(configured.enabled())
  50:                     .weight(configured.weight())
  51:                     .collectionIntervalSeconds(seed.defaultIntervalSeconds())
  52:                     .lastStatus("NEVER_RUN")
  53:                     .apiKeyEnvVar(seed.apiKeyEnvVar())
  54:                     .build());
  55:         }
  56:     }
  57: 
  58:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
  59:     public List<SentimentProviderConfig> findAll() {
  60:         return repository.findAllByOrderByDisplayNameAsc();
  61:     }
  62: 
  63:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
  64:     public SentimentProviderConfig require(String provider) {
  65:         return repository.findByProviderCodeIgnoreCase(SentimentProviderName.normalize(provider))
  66:                 .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("Unknown sentiment provider: " + provider));
  67:     }
  68: 
  69:     @Transactional
  70:     public SentimentProviderConfig update(String provider, SentimentProviderUpdateRequest request) {
  71:         SentimentProviderConfig config = require(provider);
  72:         if (request.enabled() != null) config.setEnabled(request.enabled());
  73:         if (request.weight() != null) config.setWeight(request.weight().max(BigDecimal.ZERO));
  74:         if (request.collectionIntervalSeconds() != null) {
  75:             config.setCollectionIntervalSeconds(Math.max(60, request.collectionIntervalSeconds()));
  76:         }
  77:         return repository.save(config);
  78:     }
  79: 
  80:     @Transactional
  81:     public void recordResult(String provider, String status, String message, boolean success) {
  82:         SentimentProviderConfig config = require(provider);
  83:         Instant now = Instant.now();
  84:         config.setLastCollectionAt(now);
  85:         if (success) config.setLastSuccessAt(now);
  86:         config.setLastStatus(status);
  87:         config.setLastMessage(message == null ? null : message.substring(0, Math.min(1000, message.length())));
  88:         repository.save(config);
  89:     }
  90: 
  91:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
  92:     public boolean enabled(String provider) {
  93:         return properties.enabled() && require(provider).isEnabled();
  94:     }
  95: 
  96: 
  97:     public boolean apiKeyConfigured(String provider) {
  98:         String key = properties.provider(SentimentProviderName.normalize(provider).toLowerCase()).apiKey();
  99:         SentimentProviderConfig config = require(provider);
 100:         return config.getApiKeyEnvVar() == null || (key != null && !key.isBlank());
 101:     }
 102: 
 103:     public boolean masterEnabled() {
 104:         return properties.enabled();
 105:     }
 106: 
 107:     public ProviderHealth health(SentimentProviderConfig config, Instant now) {
 108:         if (!properties.enabled() || !config.isEnabled()) {
 109:             return new ProviderHealth("DISABLED", false, 0, "Provider is disabled");
 110:         }
 111:         Instant success = config.getLastSuccessAt();
 112:         if (success == null) {
 113:             String state = "NEVER_RUN".equals(config.getLastStatus()) ? "STALE" : "DOWN";
 114:             return new ProviderHealth(state, false, -1,
 115:                     "No successful collection has been recorded");
 116:         }
 117:         java.time.Duration age = java.time.Duration.between(success, now);
 118:         long hours = Math.max(0, age.toHours());
 119:         if (age.compareTo(properties.health().downAfter()) >= 0) {
 120:             return new ProviderHealth("DOWN", false, hours,
 121:                     "No successful collection for " + hours + " hours");
 122:         }
 123:         if (age.compareTo(properties.health().staleAfter()) >= 0) {
 124:             return new ProviderHealth("STALE", false, hours,
 125:                     "Latest successful data is " + hours + " hours old");
 126:         }
 127:         if ("FAILED".equals(config.getLastStatus())) {
 128:             return new ProviderHealth("DEGRADED", true, hours,
 129:                     "Latest attempt failed; recent successful data is still active");
 130:         }
 131:         return new ProviderHealth("HEALTHY", true, hours, "Provider is collecting normally");
 132:     }
 133: 
 134:     public boolean contributes(SentimentProviderConfig config, Instant now) {
 135:         return health(config, now).contributing();
 136:     }
 137: 
 138:     public record ProviderHealth(String status, boolean contributing, long hoursSinceSuccess, String message) {}
 139: 
 140: 
 141:     private record Seed(String displayName, String apiKeyEnvVar, long defaultIntervalSeconds) {}
 142: }

  59:         for (com.crypto.domain.SentimentProviderConfig provider : providerConfigService.findAll()) {
  60:             if (!provider.isEnabled() || "MANUAL_NEWS".equals(provider.getProviderCode())) continue;
  61:             result.putAll(collectProvider(provider.getProviderCode()));
  62:         }
  63:         return result;
  64:     }
  65: 
  66:     public Map<String, String> collectProvider(String providerCode) {
  67:         String provider = SentimentProviderName.normalize(providerCode);
  68:         Map<String, String> status = new LinkedHashMap<>();
  69:         if (!properties.enabled()) {
  70:             status.put(provider, "sentiment disabled");
  71:             return status;
  72:         }
  73:         com.crypto.domain.SentimentProviderConfig config = providerConfigService.require(provider);
  74:         if (!config.isEnabled()) {
  75:             status.put(provider, "disabled");
  76:             return status;
  77:         }
  78:         switch (provider) {
  79:             case "FEAR_GREED" -> collectFearGreed(status);
  80:             case "CRYPTOPANIC" -> collectCryptoPanic(status);
  81:             case "NEWS_API" -> collectNewsApi(status);
  82:             case "REDDIT", "X", "BINANCE_ANNOUNCEMENT", "WHALE_ALERT" -> status.put(provider, configuredOnly(provider.toLowerCase()));
  83:             default -> status.put(provider, "no automatic collector");
  84:         }
  85:         String message = status.getOrDefault(provider, "completed");
  86:         boolean success = !message.startsWith("failed") && !message.contains("missing") && !message.contains("not configured");
  87:         String state = success ? "SUCCESS" : (message.contains("missing") ? "MISSING_KEY" : message.contains("not configured") ? "NOT_IMPLEMENTED" : "FAILED");
  88:         providerConfigService.recordResult(provider, state, message, success);
  89:         return status;
  90:     }
  91: 
  92:     private void collectFearGreed(Map<String, String> status) {
  93:         SentimentProperties.Provider config = properties.provider("fear_greed");
  94:         if (!providerConfigService.enabled("FEAR_GREED")) { status.put("FEAR_GREED", "disabled"); return; }
  95:         try {
  96:             String base = blankToDefault(config.baseUrl(), "https://api.alternative.me");
  97:             JsonNode root = getJson(base + "/fng/?limit=1&format=json", null);
  98:             JsonNode item = root.path("data").path(0);
  99:             int value = item.path("value").asInt(50);
 100:             BigDecimal score = BigDecimal.valueOf((value - 50) / 50.0)
 101:                     .max(BigDecimal.valueOf(-1)).min(BigDecimal.ONE);
 102:             String classification = item.path("value_classification").asText("Unknown");
 103:             Instant observedAt = item.hasNonNull("timestamp")
 104:                     ? Instant.ofEpochSecond(item.path("timestamp").asLong())
 105:                     : Instant.now();
 106: 
 107:             for (String symbol : symbols()) {
 108:                 sentimentService.saveProviderScore(
 109:                         symbol,
 110:                         "FEAR_GREED",
 111:                         score,
 112:                         BigDecimal.valueOf(0.90),
 113:                         "Fear & Greed=" + value + " (" + classification + ")",
 114:                         observedAt
 115:                 );
 116:             }
 117:             status.put("FEAR_GREED", "collected for " + symbols().size() + " symbols");
 118:         } catch (Exception ex) {
 119:             log.warn("Fear & Greed collection failed: {}", ex.getMessage());
 120:             status.put("FEAR_GREED", "failed: " + ex.getMessage());
 121:         }
 122:     }
 123: 
 124:     private void collectCryptoPanic(Map<String, String> status) {
 125:         SentimentProperties.Provider config = properties.provider("cryptopanic");
 126:         if (!providerConfigService.enabled("CRYPTOPANIC")) { status.put("CRYPTOPANIC", "disabled"); return; }
 127:         if (isBlank(config.apiKey())) {
 128:             status.put("CRYPTOPANIC", "enabled but CRYPTOPANIC_API_KEY is missing");
 129:             return;
 130:         }

 225: 
 226:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();

 229:         BigDecimal sentiment = sentimentOverview.weightedScore();

 231:         recordReplayStage(profiler, ReplayAnalysisStage.SENTIMENT, profileStartedNs);
 232: 
 233:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();

 235:         boolean fundamentalAvailable = fundamentalService.isAvailable(fundamental, signalGeneratedAt);
 236:         recordReplayStage(profiler, ReplayAnalysisStage.FUNDAMENTALS, profileStartedNs);
 237: 
 238:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 239:         IndicatorSnapshot previous = previousSnapshot(i);
 240:         recordReplayStage(profiler, ReplayAnalysisStage.PREVIOUS_SNAPSHOT, profileStartedNs);
 241: 
 242:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 243:         TrendStructureResult trendStructure = trendStructureService.evaluate(i);
 244:         recordReplayStage(profiler, ReplayAnalysisStage.TREND_STRUCTURE, profileStartedNs);
 245: 
 246:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 247:         MovingAverageBreakdown movingAverages = movingAverageScore(i, previous, trendStructure);
 248:         MomentumBreakdown momentumBreakdown = momentumScore(i);
 249:         BandsVolumeBreakdown bandsVolume = bandsVolumeScore(i, previous);
 250:         int trend = movingAverages.total();
 251:         int volume = bandsVolume.total();
 252:         int momentum = momentumBreakdown.total();
 253:         int sentimentPoints = sentimentAvailable ? sentimentScore(sentiment) : 0;
 254:         FundamentalScoreResult fundamentalBreakdown = fundamentalScoringService.score(fundamentalAvailable ? fundamental : null);
 255:         int fundamentals = fundamentalAvailable ? fundamentalBreakdown.total() : 0;
 256:         recordReplayStage(profiler, ReplayAnalysisStage.BASE_SCORING, profileStartedNs);
 257: 
 258:         int baseRawTotal = trend + volume + momentum + sentimentPoints + fundamentals;
 259:         int baseMaximumAvailableScore = MAX_TREND_SCORE
 260:                 + MAX_VOLUME_SCORE
 261:                 + MAX_MOMENTUM_SCORE
 262:                 + (fundamentalAvailable ? MAX_FUNDAMENTAL_SCORE : 0)
 263:                 + (sentimentAvailable ? MAX_SENTIMENT_SCORE : 0);
 264: 
 265:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 266:         AtrRiskAssessment atrRisk = atrRiskService.assess(i);
 267:         recordReplayStage(profiler, ReplayAnalysisStage.ATR_RISK, profileStartedNs);
 268: 
 269:         profileStartedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();

 272:                 symbol, i.intervalCode(), i.candleOpenTime(), detectedRegimeAssessment);
 273:         MarketRegimeAssessment regimeAssessment = regimeState.effectiveAssessment();
 274:         MarketRegime marketRegime = regimeAssessment.regime();

1014:                 + "; normalized=" + normalizedTotal + "/100");

1017:         }

1020: 
1021:     private boolean hasUsableSentimentCoverage(boolean sentimentEnabled, SentimentOverview overview) {
1022:         if (!sentimentEnabled || overview == null || overview.providers() == null) return false;
1023:         var enabled = overview.providers().stream().filter(provider -> provider.enabled()).toList();
1024:         if (enabled.isEmpty()) return false;
1025: 
1026:         var usable = enabled.stream()
1027:                 .filter(provider -> provider.sampleCount() > 0)
1028:                 .filter(provider -> provider.confidence() != null
1029:                         && provider.confidence().compareTo(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.40)) >= 0)
1030:                 .filter(provider -> provider.effectiveWeight() != null
1031:                         && provider.effectiveWeight().compareTo(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.05)) >= 0)
1032:                 .toList();
1033:         if (usable.isEmpty()) return false;
1034: 
1035:         double coverage = usable.size() / (double) enabled.size();
1036:         BigDecimal totalEffectiveWeight = usable.stream()
1037:                 .map(provider -> provider.effectiveWeight())
1038:                 .reduce(BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal::add);
1039:         return coverage >= 0.50 && totalEffectiveWeight.compareTo(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.05)) >= 0;
1040:     }
1041: 
1042:     private String serializeExcludedCategories(boolean sentimentEnabled, boolean sentimentAvailable,
1043:                                                boolean fundamentalAvailable, MarketFundamental fundamental) {
1044:         Map<String, String> excluded = new LinkedHashMap<>();
1045:         if (!sentimentAvailable) {
1046:             excluded.put("SENTIMENT", sentimentEnabled
1047:                     ? "No healthy contributing provider; maximum excluded"
1048:                     : "Feature disabled; maximum excluded");
1049:         }
1050:         if (!fundamentalAvailable) {
1051:             excluded.put("FUNDAMENTALS", fundamental == null
1052:                     ? "No collected record; maximum excluded"
1053:                     : "Record stale or missing required fields; maximum excluded");
1054:         }
1055:         try {
1056:             return objectMapper.writeValueAsString(excluded);
1057:         } catch (JsonProcessingException exception) {
1058:             return "{}";
1059:         }
1060:     }

 149:      * become legacy candle-price fallback. Historical pre-collector windows retain
 150:      * their established fallback. This reads immutable evidence, never live claims. */
 151:     public void assertReplayEvidence(String symbol,Instant from,Instant to) {
 152:         Long pending=jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM market_price_event WHERE symbol=? AND observed_at BETWEEN ? AND ? AND delivery_status='PENDING'",
 153:             Long.class,symbol,Timestamp.from(from),Timestamp.from(to));
 154:         if(pending!=null && pending>0)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 Replay contains unconfirmed Production price outcomes; reconcile before claiming parity");
 155:         // FIX-153 OFF Replay never queries collector-era activation tables.
 156:         if("OFF".equals(mode))return;
 157:         Long cutovers=jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM shared_market_cutover_history WHERE symbol=? AND cutover_at<=?",Long.class,symbol,Timestamp.from(to));
 158:         if(cutovers!=null && cutovers>0) {
 159:             Long applied=jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM market_price_event WHERE symbol=? AND observed_at BETWEEN ? AND ? AND delivery_status IN ('LEGACY','APPLIED')",
 160:                 Long.class,symbol,Timestamp.from(from),Timestamp.from(to));
 161:             if(applied==null || applied==0)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 no applied price evidence in collector-era Replay window; signal-price fallback is not Production reproduction");
 162:         }
 163:     }

   1: package com.crypto.execution.service;

   3: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;

  16: /**
  17:  * Thread-bound data/persistence override used only by Administration historical replay.
  18:  *
  19:  * The execution algorithms remain the production services. While this scope is active,
  20:  * their signal lookups are resolved exclusively from replay-generated signals as-of the
  21:  * current replay timestamp, and opportunity persistence is redirected to the supplied
  22:  * shadow sink. Production repositories are never read/written by the scoped execution path.
  23:  */
  24: @Component
  25: public class ExecutionReplayScope {
  26: 
  27:     /** FIX-109: Production parity is the default replay contract. Experimental mode is
  28:      * explicit so replay-only candidate rules can never contaminate a parity result. */
  29:     public enum ReplayLogicMode { PRODUCTION_PARITY, EXPERIMENTAL }
  30: 
  31:     private final ThreadLocal<State> state = new ThreadLocal<>();
  32: 
  33:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink) {
  34:         return open(runId, replaySignals, opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY);
  35:     }
  36: 
  37:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink,
  38:                       ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
  39:         if (state.get() != null) throw new IllegalStateException("Execution replay scope is already active on this thread");
  40:         List<TradeSignal> immutable = replaySignals == null ? List.of() : replaySignals.stream()
  41:                 .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null)
  42:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt))
  43:                 .toList();
  44:         state.set(new State(runId, immutable, opportunitySink, logicMode == null ? ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY : logicMode));
  45:         return new Scope();
  46:     }
  47: 
  48:     /** FIX-122: run-local executed boundaries, independent of Production ledger/history. */
  49:     public void recordStop(String symbol, long positionId, Instant at, String reason) {
  50:         if (!"STOP_LOSS".equals(reason)) return;
  51:         State s=required();
  52:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary previous=s.stops.get(symbol);
  53:         if (previous==null || !at.isBefore(previous.executedAt()))
  54:             s.stops.put(symbol,new StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary(positionId,at));
  55:     }
  56:     public StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary stopBoundary(String symbol, Instant asOf) {
  57:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary b=required().stops.get(symbol);
  58:         return b!=null && !b.executedAt().isAfter(asOf) ? b : null;
  59:     }
  60:     public Instant referenceInstant() { return required().reference; }
  61:     public boolean fix122Enabled() { return required().fix122Enabled; }
  62:     public void fix122Enabled(boolean value) { required().fix122Enabled=value; }
  63: 
  64:     public boolean active() { return state.get() != null; }
  65:     public long runId() { return required().runId; }
  66:     public ReplayLogicMode logicMode() { return required().logicMode; }
  67:     public boolean experimental() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.EXPERIMENTAL; }
  68:     public boolean productionParity() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY; }
  69: 
  70:     public void reference(Instant reference) { required().reference = reference; }
  71: 
  72:     /** FIX-056: expose the exact latest replay market-price event to the shared execution-price authority. */
  73:     public void referencePrice(String symbol, Instant observedAt, java.math.BigDecimal price) {
  74:         State s = required();

  84:     /** FIX-112A: Replay-local execution ledger keyed to the exact triggering signal. */
  85:     public boolean entryConsumed(Long signalId) {

  92:     }
  93: 
  94:     /**
  95:      * Adds a freshly generated replay signal to the as-of context stream. Signal generation
  96:      * uses this incrementally so the production confluence/BTC services can resolve only
  97:      * context that had genuinely been generated by the current replay timestamp.
  98:      */
  99:     public void appendSignal(TradeSignal signal) {
 100:         if (signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null) return;
 101:         State s = required();
 102:         s.signals.add(signal);
 103:         s.signals.sort(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt));
 104:     }
 105: 
 106:     public List<TradeSignal> recent(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, int limit) {
 107:         State s = required();
 108:         Instant ref = reference != null ? reference : s.reference;
 109:         if (ref == null) return List.of();
 110:         return s.signals.stream()
 111:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 112:                 .filter(x -> !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(ref))
 113:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt).reversed())
 114:                 .limit(limit)
 115:                 .toList();
 116:     }
 117: 
 118:     public Optional<TradeSignal> latestAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
 119:         return recent(symbol, interval, reference, 1).stream().findFirst();
 120:     }
 121: 
 122:     /**
 123:      * FIX-141: Replay-side equivalent of
 124:      * TradeSignalRepository#findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndCandleOpenTimeLessThanEqualAndGeneratedAtLessThanEqualOrderByCandleOpenTimeDesc.
 125:      * Selects by candle open time (close-time ordering), not generatedAt, so Replay cannot diverge
 126:      * from Production on which BTC snapshot it picks. candleOpenTimeBound is the caller's look-ahead
 127:      * bound (reference minus the interval duration); generatedAt must also be at or before reference.
 128:      */
 129:     public Optional<TradeSignal> latestClosedAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, Instant candleOpenTimeBound) {
 130:         State s = required();
 131:         if (reference == null || candleOpenTimeBound == null) return Optional.empty();
 132:         return s.signals.stream()
 133:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 134:                 .filter(x -> x.getGeneratedAt() != null && !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(reference))
 135:                 .filter(x -> x.getCandleOpenTime() != null && !x.getCandleOpenTime().isAfter(candleOpenTimeBound))
 136:                 .max(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getCandleOpenTime));
 137:     }
 138: 
 139:     public Optional<TradeSignal> previousBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
 140:         State s = required();
 141:         return s.signals.stream()
 142:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 143:                 .filter(x -> x.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(reference))
 144:                 .max(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt));
 145:     }
 146: 
 147:     public Optional<ExecutionOpportunity> currentOpportunity(String symbol, List<String> statuses) {
 148:         ExecutionOpportunity o = required().opportunity;
 149:         if (o == null || !symbol.equals(o.getSymbol())) return Optional.empty();
 150:         if (statuses != null && !statuses.isEmpty() && !statuses.contains(o.getStatus())) return Optional.empty();
 151:         return Optional.of(o);
 152:     }
 153: 
 154:     public ExecutionOpportunity saveOpportunity(ExecutionOpportunity opportunity) {
 155:         State s = required();
 156:         s.opportunity = opportunity;
 157:         if (s.opportunitySink != null) s.opportunitySink.accept(opportunity);
 158:         return opportunity;
 159:     }
 160: 
 161:     public final class Scope implements AutoCloseable {
 162:         private boolean closed;
 163:         @Override public void close() {
 164:             if (!closed) {
 165:                 state.remove();
 166:                 closed = true;
 167:             }
 168:         }
 169:     }
 170: 
 171:     private State required() {
 172:         State s = state.get();
 173:         if (s == null) throw new IllegalStateException("No Administration execution replay scope is active");
 174:         return s;
 175:     }
