   9: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
  10: 
  11: import java.sql.Timestamp;
  12: import java.time.Duration;
  13: import java.time.Instant;
  14: import java.util.concurrent.Executor;
  15: import java.util.concurrent.RejectedExecutionException;
  16: 
  17: /**
  18:  * Best-effort, append-only FIX-154 evidence. It snapshots values then writes
  19:  * on a bounded dedicated executor: an audit insert never nests a database
  20:  * connection inside an execution transaction and never changes a BUY result.
  21:  */
  22: @Slf4j
  23: @Component
  24: public class BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore {
  25:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbc;
  26:     private final Executor executor;
  27:     private final Fix154Metrics metrics;
  28:     @Autowired(required = false)
  29:     private ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
  30: 
  31:     public BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore(JdbcTemplate jdbc, @Qualifier("fix154AuditExecutor") Executor executor,
  32:                                       Fix154Metrics metrics) {
  33:         this.jdbc = jdbc;
  34:         this.executor = executor;
  35:         this.metrics = metrics;
  36:     }
  37: 
  38:     public void record(TradeSignal reference, String path,
  39:                        HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment,
  40:                        String finalResult, String reason) {
  41:         // Replay signals are JDBC-generated and have no entity id. Replay parity
  42:         // uses its own evidence tables; never write a misleading Production audit.
  43:         if (reference == null || assessment == null || reference.getId() == null
  44:                 || (replayScope != null && replayScope.active())) return;
  45:         AuditPayload payload = AuditPayload.from(reference, path, assessment, finalResult, reason);
  46:         try {
  47:             executor.execute(() -> persist(payload));
  48:         } catch (RejectedExecutionException rejected) {
  49:             metrics.auditWriteFailed();
  50:             log.warn("FIX-154 assessment audit queue is full; BUY decision remains unchanged: path={}, referenceSignalId={}",
  51:                     path, reference.getId());
  52:         }
  53:     }
  54: 
  55:     private void persist(AuditPayload p) {
  56:         try {
  57:             jdbc.update("""
  58:                     INSERT INTO buy_htf_assessment_audit (
  59:                       reference_signal_id, evaluation_path, evaluated_at, replay_mode,
  60:                       one_hour_signal_id, one_hour_raw_decision, one_hour_effective_decision,
  61:                       one_hour_candle_close_time, one_hour_age_seconds, one_hour_data_quality_valid, one_hour_usability_reason,
  62:                       five_minute_signal_id, five_minute_raw_decision, five_minute_effective_decision,
  63:                       five_minute_candle_close_time, five_minute_age_seconds, five_minute_data_quality_valid, five_minute_usability_reason,
  64:                       confidence_threshold, assessment_band, hard_confirmation_present, max_initial_percent,
  65:                       legacy_veto_result, final_result, final_reason)
  66:                     VALUES (?, ?, ?, FALSE, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
  67:                     """,
  68:                     p.referenceId, p.path, Timestamp.from(p.evaluatedAt),
  69:                     p.one.id, p.one.decision, p.effectiveOne, timestamp(p.one.candleClose), p.one.ageSeconds, p.one.quality, p.one.usability,
  70:                     p.five.id, p.five.decision, p.effectiveFive, timestamp(p.five.candleClose), p.five.ageSeconds, p.five.quality, p.five.usability,
  71:                     HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.HARD_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD, p.band, p.hardConfirmationPresent, p.maxInitialPercent,
  72:                     p.legacyVeto, p.finalResult, p.reason);
  73:         } catch (Exception e) {
  74:             metrics.auditWriteFailed();
  75:             log.warn("FIX-154 assessment audit was not recorded; BUY decision remains unchanged: path={}, referenceSignalId={}",
  76:                     p.path, p.referenceId, e);
  77:         }
  78:     }
  79: 
  80:     private static Timestamp timestamp(Instant value) { return value == null ? null : Timestamp.from(value); }
  81: 
  82:     private record Context(Long id, String decision, Instant candleClose, Long ageSeconds, Boolean quality, String usability) {
  83:         static Context from(TradeSignal signal, Instant evaluatedAt) {
  84:             if (signal == null) return new Context(null, null, null, null, null, "MISSING");
  85:             Instant close = deriveCandleClose(signal);
  86:             Long age = close == null ? null : Math.max(0L, Duration.between(close, evaluatedAt).getSeconds());
  87:             return new Context(signal.getId(), signal.getDecision() == null ? null : signal.getDecision().name(), close, age,
  88:                     signal.getDataQualityValid(), Boolean.TRUE.equals(signal.getDataQualityValid()) ? "AVAILABLE" : "QUALITY_UNKNOWN");
  89:         }
  90:     }
  91:     private record AuditPayload(Long referenceId, String path, Instant evaluatedAt, Context one, Context five,
  92:                                 String effectiveOne, String effectiveFive, String band, boolean hardConfirmationPresent,
  93:                                 Integer maxInitialPercent, String legacyVeto, String finalResult, String reason) {
  94:         static AuditPayload from(TradeSignal reference, String path, HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment,
  95:                                  String finalResult, String reason) {
  96:             Instant evaluated = reference.getGeneratedAt() == null ? Instant.now() : reference.getGeneratedAt();
  97:             String legacy = "5M_BEARISH_VETO".equals(assessment.code()) || "1H_BEARISH_VETO".equals(assessment.code())
  98:                     ? assessment.code() : null;
  99:             return new AuditPayload(reference.getId(), path, evaluated,
 100:                     Context.from(assessment.rawOneHour(), evaluated), Context.from(assessment.rawFiveMinute(), evaluated),
 101:                     assessment.effectiveOneHour().name(), assessment.effectiveFiveMinute().name(), assessment.band().name(),
 102:                     assessment.hardConfirmationPresent(), assessment.maxInitialPercent(), legacy, finalResult, trim(reason));
 103:         }
 104:     }
 105:     private static Instant deriveCandleClose(TradeSignal signal) {
 106:         if (signal.getCandleOpenTime() == null || signal.getInterval() == null) return null;
 107:         long seconds = switch (signal.getInterval()) { case "1m" -> 60; case "5m" -> 300; case "1h" -> 3600; default -> 0; };
 108:         return seconds == 0 ? null : signal.getCandleOpenTime().plusSeconds(seconds).minusMillis(1);
 109:     }
 110:     private static String trim(String value) { return value == null ? null : value.length() <= 1000 ? value : value.substring(0, 1000); }
 111: }

   1: package com.crypto.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.BtcContextStatus;
   4: import com.crypto.domain.SignalDecision;
   5: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   6: import com.crypto.repository.TradeSignalRepository;

  11: import com.crypto.wallet.domain.WalletSettings;
  12: import com.crypto.wallet.repository.WalletSettingsRepository;
  13: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
  14: import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
  15: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
  16: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
  17: 
  18: import java.math.BigDecimal;
  19: import java.time.Duration;
  20: import java.time.Instant;
  21: 
  22: /**
  23:  * Execution-only multi-timeframe validation.
  24:  *
  25:  * Frozen responsibilities:
  26:  *  - 1m: the only normal execution timeframe
  27:  *  - 5m: tactical confirmation
  28:  *  - 1h: strategic filter/veto
  29:  *
  30:  * Execution profiles affect only entry eligibility and position percentage.
  31:  * They never modify indicators, scores, or FinalDecisionService output.
  32:  */
  33: @Slf4j
  34: @Service
  35: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  36: public class TradeExecutionValidationService {
  37: 
  38:     private static final String EXECUTION_INTERVAL = "1m";
  39:     private static final String CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL = "5m";
  40:     private static final String TREND_INTERVAL = "1h";
  41:     private static final Duration ONE_MINUTE_TRANSITION_MAX_AGE = Duration.ofMinutes(10);
  42:     private static final Duration FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE = Duration.ofMinutes(20);
  43:     private static final Duration ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE = Duration.ofHours(3);
  44: 
  45:     // FIX-11G: this mirrors the existing global CHASE_ENTRY_CUTOFF in
  46:     // ExecutionIntelligenceService. We deliberately reuse the proven execution-quality
  47:     // boundary instead of inventing a new confidence/quality threshold for this one route.
  48:     // The downstream global Entry Quality guard still remains authoritative and re-checks it.
  49:     private static final int BALANCED_TRANSITIONAL_MIN_ENTRY_QUALITY = 50;
  50: 
  51:     private final TradeSignalRepository signalRepository;
  52:     private final WalletSettingsRepository settingsRepository;
  53:     private final EntryConsumptionPolicy entryConsumptionPolicy;
  54:     private final HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService htfAssessment;
  55:     @Autowired(required = false)
  56:     private ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
  57:     @Autowired(required = false)
  58:     private BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore htfAuditStore;
  59:     @Autowired(required = false)
  60:     private Fix154Metrics fix154Metrics;
  61: 
  62:     public ValidationResult validateBuy(TradeSignal executionSignal, int entryQualityScore) {
  63:         ValidationResult structural = validateBaseSignal(executionSignal, true);
  64:         if (!structural.allowed()) return structural;
  65: 
  66:         WalletSettings settings = settings();
  67:         if (settings.isRequireNewBuyTransition()) {
  68:             TradeSignal previous = previousSignal(executionSignal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
  69:             // FIX-112A: a bullish label alone is not a consumed entry. Only keep
  70:             // BUY_CONTINUATION protection when that exact previous signal has an
  71:             // executed BUY (Production) or executed shadow BUY (Replay).
  72:             if (isFresh(previous, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_MINUTE_TRANSITION_MAX_AGE)
  73:                     && isBullish(previous.getDecision())
  74:                     && entryConsumptionPolicy.resolve(previous.getId()) != EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED) {
  75:                 return ValidationResult.reject("BUY_CONTINUATION",
  76:                         "BUY was not executed because a recent bullish opportunity for this symbol was already consumed. "
  77:                                 + "A new WATCH/NEUTRAL/SELL -> BUY transition is required.");
  78:             }
  79:         }
  80: 
  81:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
  82:         if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
  83:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
  84:                     "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
  85:         }
  86: 
  87:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
  88:         if (!isFresh(oneHour, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
  89:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT",
  90:                     "No fresh 1h strategic context was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
  91:         }
  92: 
  93:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment=htfAssessment.assess(fiveMinute,true,oneHour,true);
  94:         if(!assessment.allowed()) {
  95:             recordAssessmentMetric(assessment);
  96:             audit(executionSignal, "DIRECT_BUY", assessment, assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY.");
  97:             return ValidationResult.reject(assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY.");
  98:         }
  99: 
 100:         ExecutionProfile profile = ExecutionProfile.from(settings.getExecutionProfile());
 101:         ValidationResult profileResult = switch (profile) {
 102:             case CONSERVATIVE -> conservativeBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 103:             // FIX-112B: only the fresh direct-BUY BALANCED path receives the narrow
 104:             // 5m-NEUTRAL exception. validateBuyContext() and AGGRESSIVE fallback keep
 105:             // calling balancedBuy() directly so their proven Production behavior is unchanged.
 106:             case BALANCED -> balancedBuyWithNeutralFiveException(
 107:                     executionSignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour(), entryQualityScore);
 108:             case AGGRESSIVE -> aggressiveBuy(executionSignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 109:         };
 110:         ValidationResult result = cap(profileResult, assessment);
 111:         audit(executionSignal, "DIRECT_BUY", assessment, result.code(), result.explanation());
 112:         return result;
 113:     }
 114: 
 115:     /**
 116:      * FIX-021 / accumulated-evidence authority parity:
 117:      * Validate only the current 5m/1h execution authority using the same configured
 118:      * execution profile as a normal direct BUY. This intentionally skips the 1m BUY
 119:      * transition requirement because accumulated evidence may be triggered by a WATCH,
 120:      * but it must never be allowed to use weaker higher-timeframe authority than the

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/service/RegimeStateService.java lines 1-160 =====
   1: package com.crypto.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.MarketRegime;
   4: import com.crypto.dto.MarketRegimeAssessment;
   5: import com.crypto.execution.service.ExecutionReplayScope;
   6: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
   7: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
   8: 
   9: import java.time.Instant;
  10: import java.util.HashMap;
  11: import java.util.List;
  12: import java.util.Map;
  13: 
  14: /**
  15:  * FIX-091 / Fix 4 + FIX-109: Experimental-Replay-only regime persistence state machine.
  16:  * Production behavior is intentionally untouched until the parity gate is passed.
  17:  * State is keyed by replay run + symbol + interval and advances only on candle timestamps.
  18:  */
  19: @Service
  20: public class RegimeStateService {
  21:     private final ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
  22:     private final int requiredCandles;
  23:     // Run id is part of the key so concurrent Replay/Test workers cannot reset or contaminate
  24:     // one another. The map contains only Replay state; Production never reads it.
  25:     private final Map<String, RegimeState> replayState = new HashMap<>();
  26: 
  27:     public RegimeStateService(ExecutionReplayScope replayScope,
  28:                               @Value("${analysis.regime.persistence-candles:3}") int requiredCandles) {
  29:         this.replayScope = replayScope;
  30:         this.requiredCandles = Math.max(2, requiredCandles);
  31:     }
  32: 
  33:     public synchronized Decision apply(String symbol, String interval, Instant candleTime,
  34:                                        MarketRegimeAssessment detectedAssessment) {
  35:         if (detectedAssessment == null || !replayScope.active() || !replayScope.experimental()) {
  36:             MarketRegime regime = detectedAssessment == null ? MarketRegime.UNKNOWN : detectedAssessment.regime();
  37:             return new Decision(detectedAssessment, regime, null, regime, 0, false);
  38:         }
  39: 
  40:         long runId = replayScope.runId();
  41:         String key = runId + "|" + symbol + "|" + interval;
  42:         RegimeState state = replayState.get(key);
  43:         MarketRegime detected = detectedAssessment.regime();
  44:         if (state == null) {
  45:             state = new RegimeState(detected, null, 0, candleTime);
  46:             replayState.put(key, state);
  47:             return new Decision(detectedAssessment, detected, null, detected, 0, false);
  48:         }
  49: 
  50:         MarketRegime confirmed = state.confirmed;
  51:         MarketRegime candidate = state.candidate;
  52:         int count = state.candidateCount;
  53:         boolean promoted = false;
  54: 
  55:         if (detected == confirmed) {
  56:             candidate = null;
  57:             count = 0;
  58:         } else if (detected == candidate) {
  59:             count++;
  60:         } else {
  61:             candidate = detected;
  62:             count = 1;
  63:         }
  64: 
  65:         if (candidate != null && count >= requiredCandles) {
  66:             confirmed = candidate;
  67:             candidate = null;
  68:             count = 0;
  69:             promoted = true;
  70:         }
  71:         replayState.put(key, new RegimeState(confirmed, candidate, count, candleTime));
  72: 
  73:         MarketRegimeAssessment effective = detected == confirmed
  74:                 ? detectedAssessment
  75:                 : new MarketRegimeAssessment(confirmed, detectedAssessment.confidence(),
  76:                     append(detectedAssessment.evidence(), "FIX-091 Replay persistence: detected=" + detected
  77:                             + ", candidate=" + candidate + ", confirmed=" + confirmed
  78:                             + ", candidateCount=" + count + "/" + requiredCandles + "."));
  79:         return new Decision(effective, detected, candidate, confirmed, count, promoted);
  80:     }
  81: 
  82:     private List<String> append(List<String> evidence, String value) {
  83:         java.util.ArrayList<String> result = new java.util.ArrayList<>(evidence == null ? List.of() : evidence);
  84:         result.add(value);
  85:         return List.copyOf(result);
  86:     }
  87: 
  88:     private record RegimeState(MarketRegime confirmed, MarketRegime candidate, int candidateCount, Instant lastCandleTime) {}
  89: 
  90:     public record Decision(MarketRegimeAssessment effectiveAssessment,
  91:                            MarketRegime detectedRegime,
  92:                            MarketRegime candidateRegime,
  93:                            MarketRegime confirmedRegime,
  94:                            int candidateCount,
  95:                            boolean promoted) {}
  96: }

===== Binary minimum policy =====
   1: package com.crypto.wallet.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.client.binance.BinanceMarketDataClient;
   4: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
   5: import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
   6: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   7: 
   8: import java.math.BigDecimal;
   9: import java.math.MathContext;
  10: import java.util.Locale;
  11: 
  12: /**
  13:  * FIX-11T minimum-executable-order guard shared by Production Paper and Replay.
  14:  *
  15:  * The project intentionally models Paper/Replay as real Binance trading with only the
  16:  * final connector missing.  Therefore a Near-TP partial harvest that Binance would reject
  17:  * for being below the symbol's minimum notional is not simulated.  No alternative exit
  18:  * size is invented: the position simply continues through its existing management logic.
  19:  *
  20:  * This class deliberately checks only the agreed minimum executable notional boundary.
  21:  * It does not add a new Near-TP-specific step-size/precision model.
  22:  */
  23: @Component
  24: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  25: @Slf4j
  26: public class BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy {
  27:     private static final MathContext MC = MathContext.DECIMAL64;
  28: 
  29:     private final BinanceMarketDataClient binanceMarketDataClient;
  30:     public Evaluation evaluate(String symbol, BigDecimal quantity, BigDecimal price) {
  31:         if (symbol == null || symbol.isBlank() || quantity == null || quantity.signum() <= 0
  32:                 || price == null || price.signum() <= 0) {
  33:             return new Evaluation(false, "INVALID_ORDER_INPUT", BigDecimal.ZERO, null);
  34:         }
  35:         String pair = symbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  36:         BigDecimal notional = quantity.multiply(price, MC);
  37:         BigDecimal minimum;
  38:         try {
  39:             minimum = binanceMarketDataClient.getMinimumExecutableNotional(pair);
  40:         } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
  41:             // Fail safe. Missing exchange constraints must never be converted into permission
  42:             // to create an order that real Binance might reject.
  43:             log.warn("FIX-11T Binance minimum executable amount unavailable: symbol={}, requestedNotional={}, error={}",
  44:                     pair, notional, ex.getMessage());
  45:             return new Evaluation(false, "BINANCE_MINIMUM_UNAVAILABLE", notional, null);
  46:         }
  47:         if (notional.compareTo(minimum) < 0) {
  48:             return new Evaluation(false, "BELOW_BINANCE_MINIMUM", notional, minimum);
  49:         }
  50:         return new Evaluation(true, "EXECUTABLE", notional, minimum);
  51:     }
  52: 
  53:     public record Evaluation(boolean executable, String code, BigDecimal requestedNotional, BigDecimal minimumNotional) {
  54:     }
  55: }

===== Binary minimum policy =====
   1: package com.crypto.wallet.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.client.binance.BinanceMarketDataClient;
   4: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
   5: import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
   6: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   7: 
   8: import java.math.BigDecimal;
   9: import java.math.MathContext;
  10: import java.util.Locale;
  11: 
  12: /**
  13:  * FIX-11T minimum-executable-order guard shared by Production Paper and Replay.
  14:  *
  15:  * The project intentionally models Paper/Replay as real Binance trading with only the
  16:  * final connector missing.  Therefore a Near-TP partial harvest that Binance would reject
  17:  * for being below the symbol's minimum notional is not simulated.  No alternative exit
  18:  * size is invented: the position simply continues through its existing management logic.
  19:  *
  20:  * This class deliberately checks only the agreed minimum executable notional boundary.
  21:  * It does not add a new Near-TP-specific step-size/precision model.
  22:  */
  23: @Component
  24: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  25: @Slf4j
  26: public class BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy {
  27:     private static final MathContext MC = MathContext.DECIMAL64;
  28: 
  29:     private final BinanceMarketDataClient binanceMarketDataClient;
  30:     public Evaluation evaluate(String symbol, BigDecimal quantity, BigDecimal price) {
  31:         if (symbol == null || symbol.isBlank() || quantity == null || quantity.signum() <= 0
  32:                 || price == null || price.signum() <= 0) {
  33:             return new Evaluation(false, "INVALID_ORDER_INPUT", BigDecimal.ZERO, null);
  34:         }
  35:         String pair = symbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  36:         BigDecimal notional = quantity.multiply(price, MC);
  37:         BigDecimal minimum;
  38:         try {
  39:             minimum = binanceMarketDataClient.getMinimumExecutableNotional(pair);
  40:         } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
  41:             // Fail safe. Missing exchange constraints must never be converted into permission
  42:             // to create an order that real Binance might reject.
  43:             log.warn("FIX-11T Binance minimum executable amount unavailable: symbol={}, requestedNotional={}, error={}",
  44:                     pair, notional, ex.getMessage());
  45:             return new Evaluation(false, "BINANCE_MINIMUM_UNAVAILABLE", notional, null);
  46:         }
  47:         if (notional.compareTo(minimum) < 0) {
  48:             return new Evaluation(false, "BELOW_BINANCE_MINIMUM", notional, minimum);
  49:         }
  50:         return new Evaluation(true, "EXECUTABLE", notional, minimum);
  51:     }
  52: 
  53:     public record Evaluation(boolean executable, String code, BigDecimal requestedNotional, BigDecimal minimumNotional) {
  54:     }
  55: }

## FINDINGS

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** RPR-0.6 is not the canonical implementation contract required by governance. The required path remains [`agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md`](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md:3), currently RPR-0.4, while RPR-0.6 explicitly supersedes only “for review.” It cannot receive governing dual-agent approval as-is.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** The “complete” registry and data boundary are inaccurate. Current Replay creates `analysis_test_run`, writes `fix122_replay_revision` before starting the worker, and incrementally writes several Replay tables. RPR-0.6 names `regression_test_run` and omits `RegressionTestService`.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** M04 does not define a safe staging design. Existing per-candle writes occur during the run, and `StopLossEvidenceStore.append()` uses `REQUIRES_NEW`, deliberately surviving an outer rollback. The specification lacks staging schema, publication transition, independent-write disposition, cancellation/crash recovery, and terminal-run recovery.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** Strict `ANALYSIS_PARITY` remains under-specified and unsafe. Replay currently builds a timeline from every loaded candle, whereas Production gates candle quality before indicators. The new contract does not define the as-of quality algorithm, candle finality convention, or a complete dependency-specific no-look-ahead rule.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** Sentiment prevents the proposed generic “historical recomputation” from proving parity. Historical analysis reads today’s mutable provider enablement, weights, and health state; it also uses a different availability rule from live analysis. No configuration history exists in the shown code. The artifact must require stored contemporaneous sentiment outcomes for an oracle, and either a versioned declared profile or refusal for simulation.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** The analysis oracle is not a sufficient approval control. `ReplayParityValidator` has no production caller or test caller and compares only 13 selected fields. RPR-0.6 does not define oracle eligibility, deliberate-difference classification, coverage, or tests demonstrating both false-pass and false-alarm detection.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** Price-event ordering cannot meet §3 as written. SQL orders by `id`, but the returned `PriceEvent` discards both `id` and `ordering_slot`; it also carries no durable commit-visibility time. RPR-0.6 requires those values yet does not register `MarketPriceEventService`/projection changes or define the refusal rule for unknown visibility.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** Isolation is not structurally complete. Current Replay and shared validation read `wallet_settings`; shared minimum-notional evaluation can call Binance HTTP and converts failure to an ordinary policy outcome. M05’s named exception does not specify propagation through current catches, async execution, or policy result handling.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** RPR-0.6’s “Production policy unchanged” claim conflicts with modifying shared execution classes. Those classes now include FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment and asynchronous audit suppression during Replay, but the registry omits the assessment service, audit store, metrics, schema checks, and policy-version treatment.

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** Resource safety is not actionable. The worker uses unqualified `@Async`; configuration defines scheduler capacity and a shared 15-connection Hikari pool, but RPR-0.6 supplies neither a named Replay executor nor measurable CPU/database/freshness budgets.

## OBJECTIONS

- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** Replace the canonical contract with a new hash-identified revision, then re-review that exact file.

- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** Add ID-mapped contracts for all writes, staging/publication, independent transactions, cancellation, crash recovery, stuck-run recovery, archive compatibility, and terminal evidence.

- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** Specify strict as-of rules per dependency—especially sentiment configuration/health, candle quality, price visibility, order book, derivatives, BTC/HTF, and FIX-154—and mandate refusal where durable evidence cannot establish parity.

- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** Define the two actual execution routes separately: live-price protection ordering and candle/signal ordering, including TP extension, stop, rebase floor, profit-lock, SELL/add suppression, Near-TP, virtual daily limits, reason codes, and virtual fill clock.

- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** Add oracle tests for known matches, every compared-field divergence, final-decision divergence, and deliberate exclusions. A bounded read-only historical comparison must measure mismatch rates before any `ANALYSIS_PARITY` label is issued.

- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** Require a named bounded Replay executor and measurable production-safety thresholds. State whether shared-class changes are permitted; if so, enumerate methods and require Production equivalence/rollback validation.

- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** I agree with the corrected peer point that absence of a frozen PDF is not yet a blocker: freezing follows dual approval. I do not treat the claimed runtime `SimpleAsyncTaskExecutor` selection as independently proven without runtime evidence; the absence of a specified Replay executor and budgets is nevertheless source-proven.

## UNKNOWNS

- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** No database, runtime logs, production systems, builds, tests, or migrations were accessed in this review.

- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** The baseline commit matches current `HEAD`, but the worktree is materially dirty; a future approval requires an exact reviewed source/config snapshot, not only the commit ID.

- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** Historical sentiment configuration and health cannot currently be reconstructed from the inspected source; the prevalence of eligible strict-parity windows is therefore unknown.
